  /**
   * Board path:
   *
   * Row 1:  0 → 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 → 6 → 7
   *                                    ↓
   * Row 2: 17 ← 16 ← 15 ← 14 ← 13 ← 12 ← 11 ← 10
   *        ↓
   * Row 3: 20 → 21 → 22 → 23 → 24 → 25 → 26 → 27
   *                                    ↓
   * Row 4: 36 ← 35 ← 34 ← 33 ← 32 ← 31 ← 30
   *
   * Two vertical spaces connect each horizontal lane.
   */ function getGridPosition(position) {
  // First horizontal lane: 0–7, left to right
  if (position >= 0 && position <= 7) {
  return {
  gridRow: 1,
  gridColumn: position + 1
  };
  }
  // First vertical connector: 8–9
  if (position >= 8 && position <= 9) {
  return {
  gridRow: position - 6,
  gridColumn: 8
  };
  }
  // Second horizontal lane: 10–17, right to left
  if (position >= 10 && position <= 17) {
  return {
  gridRow: 4,
  gridColumn: 18 - position
  };
  }
  // Second vertical connector: 18–19
  if (position >= 18 && position <= 19) {
  return {
  gridRow: position - 13,
  gridColumn: 1
  };
  }
  // Third horizontal lane: 20–27, left to right
  if (position >= 20 && position <= 27) {
  return {
  gridRow: 7,
  gridColumn: position - 19
  };
  }
  // Third vertical connector: 28–29
  if (position >= 28 && position <= 29) {
  return {
  gridRow: position - 20,
  gridColumn: 8
  };
  }
  // Final horizontal lane: 30–36, right to left
  if (position >= 30 && position <= 36) {
  return {
  gridRow: 10,
  gridColumn: 37 - position
  };
  }
  return {
  gridRow: 1,
  gridColumn: 1
  };
  }
  function getPlotColor(type) {
  switch(type){
  case "scenario":
  return "bg-[#263F52] border-[#607F91]";
  case "reward":
  return "bg-[#443D2D] border-[#99845B]";
  case "penalty":
  return "bg-[#453438] border-[#936A70]";
  default:
  return "bg-[#303D50] border-[#65758A]";
  }
  }
  function getPlotIcon(type) {
  switch(type){
  case "scenario":
  return "❖";
  case "reward":
  return "◆";
  case "penalty":
  return "⚠";
  default:
  return "▦";
  }
  }
  function getDirection(position) {
  if (position >= BOARD_FINISH_POSITION) return "";
  const current = getGridPosition(position);
  const next = getGridPosition(position + 1);
  if (next.gridColumn > current.gridColumn) return "→";
  if (next.gridColumn < current.gridColumn) return "←";
  if (next.gridRow > current.gridRow) return "↓";
  return "";
  }

  }, [
  isRolling
  ]);

  });
  }

  function PresentationPanel({ scenario, playerName, onSubmit, isVoting = false, voteResult = null, voterNames = {}, isNpcPresenter = false, npcAnswer = "", onHumanVote, onContinue, onBack, onClose }) {
  const [answer, setAnswer] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__2["useState"])("");
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__2["useState"])(false);
  const [isListening, setIsListening] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__2["useState"])(false);
  const [micMessage, setMicMessage] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__2["useState"])("");
  function stopMicrophone() {
  const w = window;
  w.__niagaQuestRecognition?.stop();
  w.__niagaQuestRecognition = null;
  setIsListening(false);
  }
  function toggleMicrophone() {
  const w = window;
  const SpeechRecognition = w.SpeechRecognition || w.webkitSpeechRecognition;
  if (!SpeechRecognition) {
  setMicMessage("Speech-to-text is not supported by this browser. You can type your solution instead.");
  return;
  }
  if (isListening) {
  stopMicrophone();
  return;
  }
  const recognition = new SpeechRecognition();
  recognition.lang = "en-MY";
  recognition.continuous = true;
  recognition.interimResults = false;
  recognition.onresult = (event)=>{
  let transcript = "";
  for(let index = event.resultIndex; index < event.results.length; index++){
  transcript += `${event.results[index][0].transcript} `;
  }
  const spokenText = transcript.trim();
  if (spokenText) {
  setAnswer((previous)=>previous.trim() ? `${previous.trim()} ${spokenText}` : spokenText);
  }
  };
  recognition.onerror = ()=>{
  setMicMessage("Microphone input stopped. Check your browser's microphone permission.");
  setIsListening(false);
  };
  recognition.onend = ()=>setIsListening(false);
  w.__niagaQuestRecognition = recognition;
  setMicMessage("Speak clearly. Your speech will be transcribed into the answer box.");
  setIsListening(true);
  recognition.start();
  }
  function submitPresentation() {
  if (!answer.trim() || submitted) return;
  if (isListening) stopMicrophone();
  setSubmitted(true);
  onSubmit(answer.trim());

  function FinalResults({ players, onRestart }) {
  const rankedPlayers = [

  function GamePage() {
  const [players, setPlayers] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(()=>initialGameState.players.map((player)=>({
  ...player
  })));
  // Start with the human player, even if the player array order changes.
  const [currentPlayerIndex, setCurrentPlayerIndex] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(()=>{
  const humanIndex = initialGameState.players.findIndex((player)=>player.type === "human");
  return humanIndex >= 0 ? humanIndex : 0;
  });
  const [turnSequence, setTurnSequence] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(0);
  const [round, setRound] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(1);
  const [theme, setTheme] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])("classic");
  const npcTurnStartedRef = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useRef"])(null);
  const rollDiceRef = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useRef"])(async ()=>{});
  const [diceResult, setDiceResult] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(null);
  const [nextPosition, setNextPosition] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(null);
  const [isRolling, setIsRolling] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(false);
  const [isMoving, setIsMoving] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(false);
  const [currentScenario, setCurrentScenario] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(null);
  const [showScenarioPopup, setShowScenarioPopup] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(true);
  const [showPresentation, setShowPresentation] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(false);
  const [plotNotice, setPlotNotice] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(null);
  // Scenario evaluation state.
  const [isVoting, setIsVoting] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(false);
  const [voteResult, setVoteResult] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(null);
  const [pendingRetry, setPendingRetry] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(null);
  // Admin tools are local prototype controls for testing game states.
  const [showAdminController, setShowAdminController] = (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useState"])(false);
  (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useEffect"])(()=>{
  const savedTheme = window.localStorage.getItem("niagaquest-theme");
  if (savedTheme !== "classic" && savedTheme !== "modern") return;
  const timer = window.setTimeout(()=>setTheme(savedTheme), 0);
  return ()=>window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, []);
  (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useEffect"])(()=>{
  window.localStorage.setItem("niagaquest-theme", theme);
  }, [
  theme
  ]);
  const activePlayer = players[currentPlayerIndex];
  const humanPlayer = players.find((player)=>player.type === "human");
  const allPlayersFinished = players.length > 0 && players.every((player)=>player.status === "finished");
  // Reopen a rejected scenario when its original player gets another turn.
  // The player retries from the same plot without rolling the dice again.
  (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useEffect"])(()=>{
  if (!pendingRetry || activePlayer?.id !== pendingRetry.playerId) return;
  const timer = window.setTimeout(()=>{
  setCurrentScenario(pendingRetry.scenario);
  setShowScenarioPopup(true);
  setShowPresentation(false);
  setIsVoting(false);
  setVoteResult(null);
  setPendingRetry(null);
  }, 0);
  return ()=>window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [
  activePlayer?.id,
  pendingRetry
  ]);
  // Automatically run NPC turns. The human player still rolls manually.
  (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useEffect"])(()=>{
  if (activePlayer?.type !== "npc" || activePlayer?.status === "finished" || currentScenario || plotNotice || isRolling || isMoving || diceResult !== null || isVoting || voteResult) {
  return;
  }
  const turnKey = `${turnSequence}:${activePlayer.id}`;
  if (npcTurnStartedRef.current === turnKey) return;
  const timer = window.setTimeout(()=>{
  if (npcTurnStartedRef.current === turnKey) return;
  npcTurnStartedRef.current = turnKey;
  void rollDiceRef.current(true);
  }, 900);
  return ()=>window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [
  activePlayer?.id,
  activePlayer?.type,
  activePlayer?.status,
  turnSequence,
  currentScenario,
  plotNotice,
  isRolling,
  isMoving,
  diceResult,
  isVoting,
  voteResult
  ]);
  function wait(milliseconds) {
  return new Promise((resolve)=>setTimeout(resolve, milliseconds));
  }
  function finishTurn() {
  setCurrentScenario(null);
  setShowScenarioPopup(false);
  setShowPresentation(false);
  setIsVoting(false);
  setVoteResult(null);
  setDiceResult(null);
  setNextPosition(null);
  setIsRolling(false);
  setIsMoving(false);
  let nextIndex = currentPlayerIndex;
  for(let offset = 1; offset <= players.length; offset += 1){
  const candidateIndex = (currentPlayerIndex + offset) % players.length;
  if (players[candidateIndex]?.status !== "finished") {
  nextIndex = candidateIndex;
  break;
  }
  }
  if (nextIndex <= currentPlayerIndex && players[nextIndex]?.status !== "finished") {
  setRound((previousRound)=>previousRound + 1);
  }
  setCurrentPlayerIndex(nextIndex);
  setTurnSequence((previous)=>previous + 1);
  }
  async function movePlayerTo(destination, fromPosition) {
  const boundedDestination = Math.max(BOARD_START_POSITION, Math.min(destination, BOARD_FINISH_POSITION));
  setNextPosition(boundedDestination);
  setIsMoving(true);
  const currentPosition = fromPosition;
  const direction = boundedDestination >= currentPosition ? 1 : -1;
  for(let position = currentPosition + direction; direction > 0 ? position <= boundedDestination : position >= boundedDestination; position += direction){
  await wait(400);
  setPlayers((previousPlayers)=>{
  const nextFinishOrder = Math.max(0, ...previousPlayers.map((player)=>player.finishOrder ?? 0)) + 1;
  return previousPlayers.map((player, index)=>{
  if (index !== currentPlayerIndex) return player;
  const reachedFinish = position === BOARD_FINISH_POSITION && player.status !== "finished";
  return {
  ...player,
  position,
  status: position === BOARD_FINISH_POSITION ? "finished" : player.status,
  finishOrder: reachedFinish ? nextFinishOrder : player.finishOrder,
  completedPaths: reachedFinish ? player.completedPaths + 1 : player.completedPaths
  };
  });
  });
  }
  setIsMoving(false);
  return boundedDestination;
  }
  async function showPlotResult(notice) {
  setPlotNotice(notice);
  await wait(2400);
  setPlotNotice(null);
  }
  async function resolveLanding(position) {
  const landedPlot = boardPlots[position - 1];
  if (!landedPlot) {
  finishTurn();
  return;
  }
  // Scenario plots pause the turn and open the scenario popup.
  if (landedPlot.type === "scenario") {
  const existingScenario = scenarios.find((scenario)=>scenario.id === landedPlot.scenarioId);
  const fallbackScenario = {
  id: landedPlot.scenarioId ?? `plot-${landedPlot.id}`,
  title: landedPlot.title,
  description: landedPlot.description,
  question: `How would you handle this situation as an entrepreneur: ${landedPlot.description}`,
  npcAnswer: "I would assess the situation, identify the main risks, consider practical alternatives and implement a solution that supports the business."
  };
  setCurrentScenario(existingScenario ?? fallbackScenario);
  setShowScenarioPopup(true);
  return;
  }
  const effect = landedPlot.effect;
  const moneyChange = effect?.money ?? 0;
  const scoreChange = effect?.score ?? 0;
  const movementChange = effect?.movement ?? 0;
  // Apply money and score effects to the active player.
  if (moneyChange !== 0 || scoreChange !== 0) {
  setPlayers((previousPlayers)=>previousPlayers.map((player, index)=>index === currentPlayerIndex ? {
  ...player,
  money: player.money + moneyChange,
  score: player.score + scoreChange
  } : player));
  await showPlotResult({
  title: landedPlot.title,
  description: landedPlot.description,
  moneyChange,
  scoreChange,
  movementChange: 0,
  kind: landedPlot.type === "reward" ? "reward" : "penalty"
  });
  }
  // Apply a normal plot's movement modifier once.
  if (movementChange !== 0) {
  const adjustedPosition = Math.max(BOARD_START_POSITION, Math.min(position + movementChange, BOARD_FINISH_POSITION));
  await showPlotResult({
  title: landedPlot.title,
  description: landedPlot.description,
  moneyChange: 0,
  scoreChange: 0,
  movementChange,
  kind: "movement"
  });
  if (adjustedPosition !== position) {
  const finalPosition = await movePlayerTo(adjustedPosition, position);
  // Do not chain another movement modifier from the plot reached by
  // this bonus movement. Resolve a scenario there if one is reached.
  const finalPlot = boardPlots[finalPosition - 1];
  if (finalPlot?.type === "scenario") {
  const matchedScenario = scenarios.find((scenario)=>scenario.id === finalPlot.scenarioId);
  setCurrentScenario(matchedScenario ?? {
  id: finalPlot.scenarioId ?? `plot-${finalPlot.id}`,
  title: finalPlot.title,
  description: finalPlot.description,
  question: `How would you handle this situation as an entrepreneur: ${finalPlot.description}`,
  npcAnswer: "I would assess the situation, identify the main risks, consider practical alternatives and implement a solution that supports the business."
  });
  setShowScenarioPopup(true);
  return;
  }
  // Resolve money and score on the space reached by bonus movement.
  // Movement modifiers are deliberately not chained.
  if (finalPlot && (finalPlot.type === "reward" || finalPlot.type === "penalty")) {
  const finalMoneyChange = finalPlot.effect?.money ?? 0;
  const finalScoreChange = finalPlot.effect?.score ?? 0;
  if (finalMoneyChange !== 0 || finalScoreChange !== 0) {
  setPlayers((previousPlayers)=>previousPlayers.map((player, index)=>index === currentPlayerIndex ? {
  ...player,
  money: player.money + finalMoneyChange,
  score: player.score + finalScoreChange
  } : player));
  await showPlotResult({
  title: finalPlot.title,
  description: finalPlot.description,
  moneyChange: finalMoneyChange,
  scoreChange: finalScoreChange,
  movementChange: 0,
  kind: finalPlot.type
  });
  }
  }
  }
  }
  finishTurn();
  }
  async function rollDice(isAutomatic = false) {
  if (activePlayer?.type === "npc" && !isAutomatic || activePlayer?.type === "human" && isAutomatic || isRolling || isMoving || diceResult !== null || currentScenario || plotNotice || !activePlayer) {
  return;
  }
  const result = Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) + 1;
  const startingPosition = activePlayer.position;
  const destination = Math.min(startingPosition + result, BOARD_FINISH_POSITION);
  // Roll first. Do not reveal the destination until the dice stops.
  setNextPosition(null);
  setIsRolling(true);
  await wait(3000);
  setDiceResult(result);
  setIsRolling(false);
  await wait(250);
  setNextPosition(destination);
  await wait(450);
  await movePlayerTo(destination, startingPosition);
  await resolveLanding(destination);
  }
  // Keep the latest roll handler available to the automatic NPC-turn effect.
  (0, __TURBOPACK__imported__module__71645__["useEffect"])(()=>{
  rollDiceRef.current = rollDice;
  });
  function createNpcVotes(answer, npcVoters) {
  const words = answer.toLowerCase().match(/[a-z]+/g) ?? [];
  const wordSet = new Set(words);
  const roleKeywords = [
  // Finance
  [
  "cost",
  "budget",
  "profit",
  "supplier",
  "negotiate",
  "expense",
  "price"
  ],
  // Marketing and customers
  [
  "customer",
  "quality",
  "satisfaction",
  "market",
  "feedback",
  "brand",
  "sales"
  ],
  // Operations
  [
  "implement",
  "process",
  "staff",
  "resource",
  "step",
  "operation",
  "manage"
  ],
  // Innovation
  [
  "innovation",
  "technology",
  "creative",
  "new",
  "different",
  "develop",
  "improve"
  ],
  // Sustainability
  [
  "sustainability",
  "environment",
  "waste",
  "ethical",
  "long",
  "responsible",
  "community"
  ]
  ];
  return npcVoters.map((voter)=>{
  const npcIndex = players.filter((player)=>player.type === "npc").findIndex((player)=>player.id === voter.id);
  const keywords = roleKeywords[Math.max(0, npcIndex)] ?? roleKeywords[0];
  const relevantTerms = keywords.filter((keyword)=>wordSet.has(keyword)).length;
  const hasReasoning = wordSet.has("because") || wordSet.has("therefore");
  const hasEnoughDetail = words.length >= 18;
  const hasAction = [
  "negotiate",
  "compare",
  "research",
  "review",
  "reduce",
  "increase",
  "plan",
  "monitor",
  "communicate",
  "invest",
  "switch",
  "develop",
  "implement",
  "improve",
  "manage"
  ].some((word)=>wordSet.has(word));
  // Each NPC weighs their own area of expertise, plus clarity and reasoning.
  const score = relevantTerms * 2 + Number(hasReasoning) + Number(hasEnoughDetail) + Number(hasAction);
  // A score of 3 or more is a positive recommendation.
  const choice = score >= 3 ? "approve" : "reject";
  return {
  voterId: voter.id,
  choice
  };
  });
  }
  function submitPresentation(answer) {
  if (!currentScenario || !activePlayer || !answer.trim()) return;
  setIsVoting(true);
  setVoteResult(null);
  const npcVoters = players.filter((player)=>player.id !== activePlayer.id && player.type === "npc");
  const generatedVotes = createNpcVotes(answer.trim(), npcVoters);
  window.setTimeout(()=>{
  const approveCount = generatedVotes.filter((vote)=>vote.choice === "approve").length;
  setVoteResult({
  votes: generatedVotes,
  approved: approveCount > generatedVotes.length / 2
  });
  setIsVoting(false);
  }, 1400);
  }
  function submitHumanVote(choice) {
  if (!currentScenario || activePlayer?.type !== "npc" || !humanPlayer || isVoting || voteResult) {
  return;
  }
  setIsVoting(true);
  const npcVoters = players.filter((player)=>player.type === "npc" && player.id !== activePlayer.id);
  const npcVotes = createNpcVotes(currentScenario.npcAnswer, npcVoters);
  const allVotes = [
  ...npcVotes,
  {
  voterId: humanPlayer.id,
  choice
  }
  ];
  window.setTimeout(()=>{
  const approveCount = allVotes.filter((vote)=>vote.choice === "approve").length;
  setVoteResult({
  votes: allVotes,
  approved: approveCount > allVotes.length / 2
  });
  setIsVoting(false);
  }, 1400);
  }
  function continueAfterVoting() {
  if (!voteResult || !currentScenario || !activePlayer) return;
  if (!voteResult.approved) {
  setPendingRetry({
  playerId: activePlayer.id,
  scenario: currentScenario
  });
  }
  // Approved: proceed to the next player.
  // Rejected: next player takes a turn; the presenter retries when their
  // turn comes around again, without moving from the scenario plot.
  finishTurn();
  }
  function applyAdminPosition(playerId, destination) {
  const selectedPlayer = players.find((player)=>player.id === playerId);
  if (!selectedPlayer || !Number.isInteger(destination) || destination < BOARD_START_POSITION || destination > BOARD_FINISH_POSITION || isRolling || isMoving) {
  return;
  }
  const isNewFinish = destination === BOARD_FINISH_POSITION && selectedPlayer.status !== "finished";
  const isLeavingFinish = destination !== BOARD_FINISH_POSITION && selectedPlayer.status === "finished";
  const nextFinishOrder = Math.max(0, ...players.map((player)=>player.finishOrder ?? 0)) + 1;
  const updatedPlayers = players.map((player)=>{
  if (player.id !== playerId) return player;
  return {
  ...player,
  position: destination,
  status: destination === BOARD_FINISH_POSITION ? "finished" : "playing",
  finishOrder: isNewFinish ? nextFinishOrder : isLeavingFinish ? null : player.finishOrder,
  completedPaths: isNewFinish ? player.completedPaths + 1 : isLeavingFinish ? Math.max(0, player.completedPaths - 1) : player.completedPaths
  };
  });
  setPlayers(updatedPlayers);
  // If the active player is forced to finish, hand control to the next
  // unfinished player. The final-results overlay appears when everyone is done.
  if (selectedPlayer.id === activePlayer?.id && destination === BOARD_FINISH_POSITION) {
  const nextIndex = updatedPlayers.findIndex((player)=>player.status !== "finished");
  setCurrentScenario(null);
  setShowScenarioPopup(false);
  setShowPresentation(false);
  setIsVoting(false);
  setVoteResult(null);
  setDiceResult(null);
  setNextPosition(null);
  if (nextIndex >= 0) {
  setCurrentPlayerIndex(nextIndex);
  setTurnSequence((previous)=>previous + 1);
  }
  }
  }
  function resetAdminPlayer(playerId) {
  const resetIndex = players.findIndex((player)=>player.id === playerId);
  setPlayers((previousPlayers)=>previousPlayers.map((player)=>player.id === playerId ? {
  ...player,
  position: BOARD_START_POSITION,
  status: "playing",
  finishOrder: null,
  completedPaths: 0
  } : player));
  if (resetIndex >= 0) {
  setCurrentPlayerIndex(resetIndex);
  setTurnSequence((previous)=>previous + 1);
  setCurrentScenario(null);
  setShowScenarioPopup(false);
  setShowPresentation(false);
  setIsVoting(false);
  setVoteResult(null);
  setDiceResult(null);
  setNextPosition(null);
  }
  }
  function restartGame() {
  const freshPlayers = initialGameState.players.map((player)=>({
  ...player
  }));
  const firstHuman = freshPlayers.findIndex((player)=>player.type === "human");
  setPlayers(freshPlayers);
  setCurrentPlayerIndex(firstHuman >= 0 ? firstHuman : 0);
  setTurnSequence(0);
  setRound(1);
  npcTurnStartedRef.current = null;
  setDiceResult(null);
  setNextPosition(null);
  setIsRolling(false);
  setIsMoving(false);
  setCurrentScenario(null);
  setShowScenarioPopup(false);
  setShowPresentation(false);
  setPlotNotice(null);
  setIsVoting(false);
  setVoteResult(null);
  setPendingRetry(null);
  setShowAdminController(false);
  }
  function continueToPresentation() {
  setShowScenarioPopup(true);
  setShowPresentation(true);
  }
  function reopenScenario() {
  setShowScenarioPopup(true);
  }
  function backToScenario() {
  setShowPresentation(false);
  setShowScenarioPopup(true);
  }
  function closePresentation() {
  setShowPresentation(false);
  setShowScenarioPopup(false);
  }
